📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Emerging microbes & infections  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-09-07  |  DOI: 10.1080/22221751.2026.2731496  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 2731496

在乌干达收到通知后四天内实施针对苏丹病毒病(SVD)的临床试验环形疫苗接种

Implementing an intra outbreak ring vaccination clinical trial for Sudan Ebola virus disease within four days of outbreak notification in Uganda.
作者： Winters Muttamba, Henry Kyobe Bosa, Misaki Wayengera, Andrew Obuku, Maria Sekimpi, Geofrey Kimbugwe, Wilber Sabiiti, Pauline Byakika-Kibwika, Noah Kiwanuka, Rebecca Nantanda
摘要中文翻译东非和中非地区面临埃博拉病毒病(EVD)的更高威胁,最近一次由邦迪布古埃博拉病毒(BDBV)引起的暴发于2026年5月15日报告发生在刚果民主共和国(DRC)和乌干达。此次BDBV暴发是在2025年1月30日乌干达宣布苏丹病毒病(SVD)暴发后不到一年发生的。在2025年SVD暴发之后,调查团队在随后的四天内展开了争分夺秒的行动,以启动针对SVD的候选药物疫苗的环形疫苗接种试验。在这四天期间,调查团队利用与试验赞助方的持续合作,激活了预融资机制,并从乌干达政府获取了实物融资。此外,团队激活了预先部署的研究方案,以确保快速获得伦理和监管批准,并迅速获取了国内的调查产品,支持性的伦理/监管体系,以及在2022年暴发期间建立的物流和数据管理系统,同时与试验和卫生部暴发响应团队建立了协作关系。最后,之前培训过的现场和社区参与人员在夜间被动员起来,接受了强化培训并迅速部署到现场。总之,前所未有的四天内完成的响应得益于在2022年SVD暴发期间建立的研究、物流和人员配置能力,这一成就为在暴发期间快速评估医疗对策提供了蓝图。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在应对在埃博拉病毒病暴发期间快速实施医疗对策的迫切需求,特别是在东非和中非地区埃博拉病毒病(EVD)带来的更高威胁背景下。
设计与对象
该研究涉及苏丹 埃博拉病毒病 的 环形疫苗接种 临床试验,该试验在 暴发 通知后四天内在乌干达进行,利用现有的 暴发 响应基础设施和协议。
核心方法
团队激活了预融资机制,确保了快速获得伦理和监管批准,并部署了之前培训过的现场人员并进行强化培训,以高效执行试验。
主要结果
调查小组在四天内成功动员并执行了环形疫苗接种试验,展示了暴发期间快速部署医疗对策的可行性。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为快速实施医疗对策的潜力的证明,强调了预先建立的 暴发 响应系统的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为未来的 暴发 提供了蓝图,能够更快地评估 疫苗 和其他医疗干预措施,这对于公共卫生准备工作至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据仅限于快速实施过程,不包括详细的结果或统计分析,导致可信度评估适中。

Implementing an intra outbreak ring vaccination clinical trial for Sudan Ebola virus disease within four days of outbreak notification in Uganda.

Authors: Winters Muttamba, Henry Kyobe Bosa, Misaki Wayengera, Andrew Obuku, Maria Sekimpi, Geofrey Kimbugwe, Wilber Sabiiti, Pauline Byakika-Kibwika, Noah Kiwanuka, Rebecca Nantanda
Original abstract (English)
East and Central Africa experiences heightened threats for Ebola Virus Disease (EVD), with the latest outbreak caused by the Bundibugyo ebolavirus (BDBV) reported in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC) and Uganda on 15 th May 2026. The BDBV outbreak comes just over a year following declaration of Sudan Virus Disease (SVD) outbreak in Uganda on 30 th January 2025. Following the 2025 outbreak, a race against the clock mobilisation occurred over the subsequent four days to launch a ring vaccination trial for a candidate vaccine against SVD. During this four-day period, the investigation team leveraged ongoing engagements with trial sponsors to activate prefinancing mechanisms and unlock in-kind financing from the government of Uganda. Further, the team activated a prepositioned research protocol to secure rapid ethics and regulatory clearance, and quickly accessed investigation product within the country, supportive ethics/regulatory systems, presence of logistical and data management systems established during the 2022 outbreak, collaborative relationship between the trial and the Ministry of Health outbreak response teams. Finally, previously trained field and community engagement personnel were mobilised overnight, offered refresher training and rapidly deployed to the field. In conclusion, the unprecedented four-day turnaround was made possible by the research, logistical and staffing capacities established during the 2022 SVD outbreak, an achievement that offers a blueprint for rapid evaluation of medical countermeasures during outbreaks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the urgent need for rapid implementation of medical countermeasures during Ebola virus disease outbreaks, particularly in light of the heightened threats posed by EVD in East and Central Africa.
Design and population
The study involved a ring vaccination clinical trial for Sudan Ebola virus disease, conducted within four days of outbreak notification in Uganda, leveraging existing outbreak response infrastructure and protocols.
Core methods
The team activated prefinancing mechanisms, secured rapid ethics and regulatory clearance, and deployed previously trained field personnel with refresher training to execute the trial efficiently.
Main results
The investigation team successfully mobilized and executed the ring vaccination trial within the four-day window, demonstrating the feasibility of rapid deployment of medical countermeasures during outbreaks.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as a demonstration of the potential for rapid implementation of medical countermeasures, highlighting the importance of pre-established systems for outbreak response.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings offer a blueprint for future outbreaks, enabling quicker evaluation of vaccines and other medical interventions, which is critical for public health preparedness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is limited to the rapid implementation process and does not include detailed outcomes or statistical analyses, resulting in a moderate confidence assessment.

刚果民主共和国 2026 年本迪布焦病毒病 暴发 期间的主动病例发现、监测 级联和响应瓶颈

Active Case Finding, Surveillance Cascade and Response Bottlenecks During the 2026 Bundibugyo Virus Disease Outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo
作者： Tambe Elvis Akem
摘要中文翻译埃博拉病毒 传播 的快速阻断取决于 监测 将警报转化为调查、分类、采样和护理,同时维持接触者随访、主动病例发现、实验室处理和边境筛查。截至 2026 年 8 月 25 日,刚果民主共和国 (DRC) 六个省的 58 个卫生区报告了 5,713 例确诊病例和 2,744 例确诊死亡。使用刚果民主共和国 2026 年本迪布焦病毒病 暴发 的国家形势报告系列进行了回顾性描述性生态分析,并根据世卫组织和非洲疾病预防控制中心的官方报告对时间顺序和操作背景进行了三角测量。截至 2026 年 8 月 25 日,在 103 个预期报告位置中,有 96 个 (93.2%) 可用,产生 2,375 条来源记录。应用严格的源可链接性规则,无需插补。中位累积警报调查覆盖率为 88.5%(IQR 79.9–94.2;n=97),抽样覆盖率为 66.7%(IQR 54.5–74.6;n=5),验证率 26.0%(IQR 20.2–74.2;n=35),接触者随访率为 78.2%(IQR 67.3–82.9;n=5)。n=90),进入点/控制点筛查率约为 98.0%(IQR 96.0–98.8;n=32)。44 个来源定义的兼容实验室样本 队列研究 报告的完成度范围为 89% 至 100%;这些观察结果不应被解释为总体实验室通量的独立测量。大多数过程指标是连续日期报告的累积状态测量值,不应被解释为独立的日常绩效。在国家优先的主要数据层次结构下,确定了 71 项合格的报告观察结果。55 项观察中,接触者随访是最低的测量过程 (77.5%),其次是 12 项观察 (16.9%) 中的警报调查、3 项观察中的抽样 (4.2%) 和 1 项观察中的进入点/控制点筛查 (1.4%);实验室处理在任何观察中都不是唯一最低的,并且没有发生联系。在全省优先敏感性分析中,接触者随访仍然占主导地位(67/94;71.3%)。积极的病例发现很早就被记录下来,后来被纳入常规卫生区 监测,但在一些高风险地区存在实施差距,并且无法量化人口覆盖率。官方报告记录了后勤限制、不安全、社区抵制和有限的信任,这些因素扰乱了现场访问、警报调查、标本采集和运输以及接触者追踪。公开报告并未一致地将经过验证或抽样的警报与相同的 队列研究 埃博拉 治疗 中心转移联系起来。接触随访成为主要的经常性可测量瓶颈。加强接触者追踪、积极的病例发现能力、现场后勤、社区信任和安全准入,再加上通过采样、隔离和转诊与 队列研究 相关的警报报告,应加强早期发现,加速 诊断 和护理,并帮助限制持续的社区 传播。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估刚果民主共和国2026年本迪布焦病毒病暴发期间主动病例发现、监测级联和响应瓶颈的有效性,强调需要改进监测和响应机制以快速阻断埃博拉病毒传播。
设计与对象
使用刚果民主共和国 2026 年本迪布焦病毒病 暴发 的国家形势报告系列进行了回顾性描述性生态分析,并根据世卫组织和非洲疾病预防控制中心的官方报告对时间顺序和操作背景进行了三角测量。截至 2026 年 8 月 25 日,该研究涵盖了六个省份 58 个卫生区的 5,713 例确诊病例和 2,744 例确诊死亡病例。
核心方法
该分析使用主要国家优先的日期级别层次结构来确定 71 项合格的报告观察结果。它应用了严格的源可链接性规则,无需插补,并检查了警报调查覆盖率、采样覆盖率、验证率、接触跟踪和进入点/控制点筛查等过程指标。
主要结果
截至 2026 年 8 月 25 日,103 个预期报告位置中的 96 个可用,产生 2,375 条来源记录。中位累积警报调查覆盖率为 88.5%(IQR 79.9–94.2;n=97),抽样覆盖率为 66.7%(IQR 54.5–74.6;n=5),验证率 26.0%(IQR 20.2–74.2;n=35),接触者随访率为 78.2%(IQR 67.3–82.9;n=5)。n=90),进入点/控制点筛查率约为 98.0%(IQR 96.0–98.8;n=32)。
解释与创新
作者解释说,在 55 项观察中(77.5%),接触随访是最低的测量过程,其次是 12 项观察(16.9%)中的警报调查,3 项观察中的抽样(4.2%),以及 1 项观察中的进入点/控制点筛查(1.4%)。这凸显了加强这些流程的必要性,以改进早期检测并限制社区 传播。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,加强接触者追踪、积极的病例发现能力、现场后勤、社区信任和安全获取的重要性,以增强 监测、加速 诊断 和护理,并限制埃博拉病毒在社区中的持续传播。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其对公共报告的依赖而受到限制,该报告并未一致地将经过验证或抽样的警报与相同的 队列研究 埃博拉 治疗 中心转移联系起来。证据是中等的,因为它基于回顾性数据和作战背景三角测量。

Active Case Finding, Surveillance Cascade and Response Bottlenecks During the 2026 Bundibugyo Virus Disease Outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo

Authors: Tambe Elvis Akem
Original abstract (English)
Rapid interruption of ebolavirus transmission depends on surveillance that converts alerts into investigation, classification, sampling and care while sustaining contact follow-up, active case finding, laboratory processing and border screening. By 25 August 2026, the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC) had reported 5,713 confirmed cases and 2,744 confirmed deaths across 58 health zones in six provinces. A retrospective descriptive ecological analysis was conducted using the national situation-report series from the 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease outbreak in the DRC, with chronology and operational context triangulated against official WHO and Africa CDC reports. Of 103 expected report positions through 25 August 2026, 96 (93.2%) were available, yielding 2,375 source-derived records. Strict source-linkability rules were applied without imputation. Median cumulative alert-investigation coverage was 88.5% (IQR 79.9–94.2; n=97), sampling coverage 66.7% (IQR 54.5–74.6; n=5), validation yield 26.0% (IQR 20.2–74.2; n=35), contact follow-up 78.2% (IQR 67.3–82.9; n=90), and point-of-entry/point-of-control screening approximately 98.0% (IQR 96.0–98.8; n=32). Reported completion within 44 source-defined compatible laboratory specimen cohorts ranged from 89% to 100%; these observations should not be interpreted as independent measures of overall laboratory throughput. Most process indicators were cumulative status measures reported at successive dates and should not be interpreted as independent daily performance. Under the primary national-first date-level hierarchy, 71 qualifying reporting observations were identified. Contact follow-up was the lowest measured process in 55 observations (77.5%), followed by alert investigation in 12 (16.9%), sampling in three (4.2%) and point-of-entry/point-of-control screening in one (1.4%); laboratory processing was not uniquely lowest in any observation, and no ties occurred. Contact follow-up remained dominant in the province-first sensitivity analysis (67/94; 71.3%). Active case finding was documented early and later incorporated into routine health-zone surveillance, but implementation gaps occurred in some high-risk areas and population-level coverage could not be quantified. Official reports documented logistical constraints, insecurity, community resistance and limited trust that disrupted field access, alert investigation, specimen collection and transport, and contact follow-up. Public reporting did not consistently link validated or sampled alerts to same-cohort Ebola treatment centre transfer. Contact follow-up emerged as the dominant recurrent measurable bottleneck. Strengthening contact follow-up, active case-finding capacity, field logistics, community trust and safe access, together with cohort-linked reporting from alert through sampling, isolation and referral, should strengthen early detection, accelerate diagnosis and care, and help limit continued community transmission.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the effectiveness of active case finding, surveillance cascade and response bottlenecks during the 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo, highlighting the need for improved surveillance and response mechanisms to rapidly interrupt ebolavirus transmission.
Design and population
A retrospective descriptive ecological analysis was conducted using the national situation-report series from the 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease outbreak in the DRC, with chronology and operational context triangulated against official WHO and Africa CDC reports. The study included 5,713 confirmed cases and 2,744 confirmed deaths across 58 health zones in six provinces by 25 August 2026.
Core methods
The analysis used a primary national-first date-level hierarchy to identify 71 qualifying reporting observations. It applied strict source-linkability rules without imputation and examined process indicators such as alert-investigation coverage, sampling coverage, validation yield, contact follow-up and point-of-entry/point-of-control screening.
Main results
By 25 August 2026, 96 out of 103 expected report positions were available, yielding 2,375 source-derived records. Median cumulative alert-investigation coverage was 88.5% (IQR 79.9–94.2; n=97), sampling coverage 66.7% (IQR 54.5–74.6; n=5), validation yield 26.0% (IQR 20.2–74.2; n=35), contact follow-up 78.2% (IQR 67.3–82.9; n=90), and point-of-entry/point-of-control screening approximately 98.0% (IQR 96.0–98.8; n=32).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that contact follow-up was the lowest measured process in 55 observations (77.5%), followed by alert investigation in 12 (16.9%), sampling in three (4.2%) and point-of-entry/point-of-control screening in one (1.4%). This highlights the need for strengthening these processes to improve early detection and limit community transmission.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the importance of strengthening contact follow-up, active case-finding capacity, field logistics, community trust and safe access to enhance surveillance, accelerate diagnosis and care, and limit continued community transmission of ebolavirus.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on public reporting, which did not consistently link validated or sampled alerts to same-cohort Ebola treatment centre transfer. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on retrospective data and operational context triangulation.

乌干达2022年埃博拉病毒病 暴发后幸存者的出院后经历:一项探索性定性研究

Post-Discharge Experiences of Survivors Following the 2022 Ebola Virus Disease Outbreak in Uganda: An Exploratory Qualitative Study
作者： Julian Natukunda, Patience A. Muwanguzi, Tom Denis Ngabirano, Bridget Atuhaire, Sarah Joselyn Nalubega, Christine Auma, Racheal Nabunya
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 埃博拉病毒病是由埃博拉病毒引起的一种危及生命的疾病,其症状在感染后两天至二十一天内显现。尽管乌干达曾多次遭遇埃博拉病毒暴发,但许多患者幸存下来后仍面临持续的挑战。因此,本研究探讨了乌干达2022年埃博拉病毒病 暴发后幸存者的出院后经历。方法 本研究是一项探索性定性研究,通过深入访谈参与者在乌干达中部的穆本德地区区域转诊医院进行。访谈为面对面形式,数据通过归纳性内容分析手动分析。共有十名男性和女性参与者,他们是穆本德地区埃博拉病毒病幸存者,且在从埃博拉治疗单元出院后至少在社区生活了六个月。结果 出现了四个主题:(i) 心理社会负担和社会排斥,(ii) 经济困难和财务稳定性的丧失,(iii) 恢复后慢性身体和健康负担,(iv) 重建生活:心理、社会和医疗恢复途径。幸存者面临显著的情感负担,如幸存者内疚、悲伤、因损失而产生的创伤以及对传播风险的焦虑。他们经历了社会孤立、污名化和歧视,这常常导致他们被排除在社区活动之外。在经济上,他们面临债务和生计丧失的困扰,同时伴有持续的健康问题。此外,幸存者还经历了慢性身体并发症,包括疼痛和疲劳,这阻碍了他们的恢复。尽管面临这些挑战,幸存者仍寻求心理、社会和医疗恢复途径,包括确认康复、来自家庭和组织的支持以及健康维护行为。支持性的医疗护理和社区援助对其身体和情感康复至关重要。结论 乌干达埃博拉病毒病幸存者在出院后面临显著的心理社会、健康、社会和经济挑战。研究结果强调了建立全面的医疗和社区支持系统以帮助幸存者恢复和改善生活质量的迫切需求。需要进一步研究长期神经学影响以及社区重新融入计划,以制定有针对性的干预措施,支持埃博拉幸存者并减少污名化和歧视。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
埃博拉病毒病 是一种由埃博拉病毒引起的危及生命的疾病,症状在感染后 2 至 21 天出现。尽管乌干达曾面临多发埃博拉病毒暴发,但许多患者幸存下来只是为了遇到持续的挑战。
设计与对象
本研究探讨了乌干达2022年埃博拉病毒病 暴发后幸存者的出院后经历。共有十名男性和女性参与者,他们是穆本德地区埃博拉病毒病幸存者,且在从埃博拉治疗单元出院后至少在社区生活了六个月。
核心方法
乌干达中部穆本德地区转诊医院进行了一项探索性定性研究,其中包括深入的参与者访谈。访谈是面对面的,数据通过归纳内容分析进行手动分析。
主要结果
出现了四个主题:(i) 心理社会负担和社会排斥,(ii) 经济困难和金融稳定丧失,(iii) 康复后的慢性身体和健康负担,以及 (iv) 重建生活:心理、社会和医疗康复途径。
解释与创新
研究结果强调迫切需要全面的医疗和社区支持系统来帮助幸存者康复和福祉。幸存者面临着巨大的情感负担,例如幸存者内疚、悲伤、损失造成的创伤以及对 传播 风险的焦虑。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果为制定有针对性的干预措施提供了信息,以支持埃博拉幸存者并减少耻辱和歧视。幸存者经历了社会孤立、耻辱和歧视,这往往导致他们被排除在社区活动之外。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的局限性。由于研究的定性性质和样本量有限,证据强度中等。

Post-Discharge Experiences of Survivors Following the 2022 Ebola Virus Disease Outbreak in Uganda: An Exploratory Qualitative Study

Authors: Julian Natukunda, Patience A. Muwanguzi, Tom Denis Ngabirano, Bridget Atuhaire, Sarah Joselyn Nalubega, Christine Auma, Racheal Nabunya
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Ebola virus disease is a life-threatening illness caused by the Ebolavirus, with symptoms manifesting two to twenty-one days after infection. Although Uganda has faced multiple Ebola outbreaks, many patients survive only to encounter persistent challenges. Therefore, this study explored the post-discharge experiences of survivors following the 2022 Ebola Virus Disease outbreak in Uganda. Methods An exploratory qualitative study comprising of in-depth participant interviews was conducted at Mubende Regional Referral Hospital in central Uganda. Interviews were face-to-face and data were analyzed manually by inductive content analysis. Ten male and female participants were Ebola Virus Disease survivors in Mubende district who had lived in the community for at least six months post-discharge from the Ebola Treatment Unit. Results Four themes emerged: (i) Psychosocial Burdens and Social Exclusion, (ii) Economic Hardship and Loss of Financial Stability, (iii) Chronic Physical and Health Burdens Post-Recovery and (iv) Rebuilding Lives: Psychological, Social, and Medical Pathways to Recovery. Survivors faced significant emotional burdens such as survivor guilt, grief, trauma from loss, and anxiety about transmission risks. They experienced social isolation, stigma, and discrimination, which often led to their exclusion from community activities. Financially, they struggled with debt and the loss of livelihoods, compounded by ongoing health issues. Additionally, survivors endured chronic physical complications, including pain and fatigue, which hindered their recovery. Despite these challenges, survivors sought psychological, social, and medical pathways to recovery, including confirmation of their recovery, support from family and organizations, and health maintenance practices. Supportive medical care and community assistance were crucial in their physical and emotional rehabilitation. Conclusion Ebola Virus Disease survivors in Uganda face significant psychosocial, health, social, and economic challenges post-discharge. The findings highlight the critical need for comprehensive medical and community-based support systems to aid survivors’ recovery and well-being. Further research on long-term neurological effects and community reintegration programmes is needed to inform targeted interventions that support Ebola survivors and reduce stigma and discrimination.

Question and background
Ebola virus disease is a life-threatening illness caused by the Ebolavirus, with symptoms manifesting two to twenty-one days after infection. Although Uganda has faced multiple Ebola outbreaks, many patients survive only to encounter persistent challenges.
Design and population
This study explored the post-discharge experiences of survivors following the 2022 Ebola Virus Disease outbreak in Uganda. Ten male and female participants were Ebola Virus Disease survivors in Mubende district who had lived in the community for at least six months post-discharge from the Ebola Treatment Unit.
Core methods
An exploratory qualitative study comprising of in-depth participant interviews was conducted at Mubende Regional Referral Hospital in central Uganda. Interviews were face-to-face and data were analyzed manually by inductive content analysis.
Main results
Four themes emerged: (i) Psychosocial Burdens and Social Exclusion, (ii) Economic Hardship and Loss of Financial Stability, (iii) Chronic Physical and Health Burdens Post-Recovery and (iv) Rebuilding Lives: Psychological, Social, and Medical Pathways to Recovery.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the critical need for comprehensive medical and community-based support systems to aid survivors’ recovery and well-being. Survivors faced significant emotional burdens such as survivor guilt, grief, trauma from loss, and anxiety about transmission risks.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the development of targeted interventions that support Ebola survivors and reduce stigma and discrimination. Survivors experienced social isolation, stigma, and discrimination, which often led to their exclusion from community activities.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study. The evidence strength is moderate due to the qualitative nature of the study and the limited sample size.

有条件护理 埃博拉病毒病 患者的意愿:对乌干达医学院临床学生风险收益认知和支持需求的定性研究

Conditional willingness to care for patients with Ebola virus disease: a qualitative study of risk-benefit perceptions and support needs of clinical students at a Ugandan medical school
作者： Bevindah Carol Nakabuubi, Racheal Nabunya, Tom Denis Ngabirano, Joyce Nankumbi, Lydia Kabiri, Edwin Kigozi, Auma Christine, Daniel Musindi, Isaac Alinda, Ammon Mark Kyokwijuka
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 临床学生是未来的卫生工作者,但由于安全、培训、监督和福利问题,他们在高传染性疾病 暴发 期间的角色仍然不确定。乌干达 2022 年关于苏丹埃博拉病毒引起的埃博拉疾病的 暴发 强调需要了解临床学生如何看待 暴发 相关护理。目的 本研究探讨了乌干达医学院临床学生护理 埃博拉病毒病 患者的意愿,并研究了感知风险、感知收益和支持需求如何影响这种意愿。方法 对乌干达坎帕拉麦克雷雷大学的临床学生进行了一项探索性描述性定性研究。有目的地选拔了15名医学和护理本科生的后期培训。数据通过深入访谈收集,经同意录音,逐字转录,去识别化并使用潜在内容分析进行分析。健康信念模型提高了解释的敏感性,并使用 COREQ 指南加强了报告。结果出现了五个相互关联的主题,表明护理意愿是有条件的,而不是简单地存在或不存在。学生们描述了基于职业责任、对患者的奉献和拯救生命的愿望的最初意愿。这种意愿受到感染 埃博拉病毒病、死亡、将感染传染给家人或同事、情绪困扰、课程中缺乏流行病准备、准备不足和福利支持薄弱等感知风险的限制。感知到的好处,包括患者生存、专业学习、暴发 经验和个人成就感,增强了意愿,但并没有压倒安全担忧。学生们确定了可靠的个人防护设备、流行病准备课程、实用的感染预防和控制培训、模拟、明确的方案、密切监督、心理社会支持、保险和公平补偿作为行动的线索,可以将意愿转化为安全参与。结论 乌干达教学医院的临床学生表现出强烈的职业责任感,但他们参与埃博拉救护的意愿取决于准备、防护、流行病准备教育和机构信任。专业职责和学习机会促进了参与,而感知到的风险和支持不足则限制了参与。医学教育项目和 暴发 响应系统应发展道德、受监督、基于能力的学生角色,并辅以实用课程、可靠的防护设备以及心理社会和福利保障。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨乌干达某医学院临床学生对照顾埃博拉病毒病患者的意愿,并分析其受到的感知风险、感知益处和支持需求的影响。
设计与对象
方法 对乌干达坎帕拉麦克雷雷大学的临床学生进行了一项探索性描述性定性研究。
核心方法
数据通过深入访谈收集,经同意录音,逐字转录,去识别化并使用潜在内容分析进行分析。
主要结果
结果出现了五个相互关联的主题,表明护理意愿是有条件的,而不是简单地存在或不存在。
解释与创新
职业责任和学习机会促进了参与,而感知风险和缺乏支持则限制了参与。
科研与公卫意义
结论 该乌干达教学医院的临床学生表现出强烈的职业责任感,但其参与埃博拉病毒护理的意愿取决于准备程度、防护措施、应对疫情的教育以及机构信任度。
局限与证据强度
摘要 简介 临床学生是未来的卫生工作者,但由于安全、培训、监督和福利问题,他们在高传染性疾病 暴发 期间的角色仍然不确定。

Conditional willingness to care for patients with Ebola virus disease: a qualitative study of risk-benefit perceptions and support needs of clinical students at a Ugandan medical school

Authors: Bevindah Carol Nakabuubi, Racheal Nabunya, Tom Denis Ngabirano, Joyce Nankumbi, Lydia Kabiri, Edwin Kigozi, Auma Christine, Daniel Musindi, Isaac Alinda, Ammon Mark Kyokwijuka
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Clinical students are a future health workforce, yet their roles during outbreaks of highly infectious diseases remain uncertain because of safety, training, supervision and welfare concerns. Uganda’s 2022 outbreak of Ebola disease caused by Sudan ebolavirus highlighted the need to understand how clinical students perceive outbreak-related care. Aim This study explored willingness to care for patients with Ebola virus disease among clinical students at a Ugandan medical school and examined how perceived risks, perceived benefits and support needs shaped that willingness. Methods An exploratory descriptive qualitative study was conducted among clinical students of Makerere University in Kampala, Uganda. Fifteen undergraduate medical and nursing students in the later years of training were purposively selected. Data were collected through in-depth interviews, audio-recorded with consent, transcribed verbatim, de-identified and analysed using latent content analysis. The Health Belief Model sensitised interpretation, and reporting was strengthened using the COREQ guidance. Results Five interrelated themes emerged, showing that willingness to care was conditional rather than simply present or absent. Students described an initial willingness grounded in professional duty, devotion to patients and the desire to save life. This willingness was restrained by perceived risks of contracting Ebola virus disease, dying, transmitting infection to family members or colleagues, emotional distress, lack of epidemic-readiness in the curriculum, inadequate preparedness and weak welfare support. Perceived benefits, including patient survival, professional learning, outbreak experience and personal fulfilment, strengthened willingness but did not override safety concerns. Students identified reliable personal protective equipment, epidemic-ready curricula, practical infection-prevention and control training, simulation, clear protocols, close supervision, psychosocial support, insurance and fair compensation as cues to action that could convert willingness into safe participation. Conclusions Clinical students in this Ugandan teaching hospital expressed a strong sense of professional responsibility, but their willingness to participate in Ebola care was conditional upon preparedness, protection, epidemic-ready education and institutional trust. Professional duty and learning opportunities promoted participation, whereas perceived risks and inadequate support limited it. Medical education programmes and outbreak-response systems should develop ethical, supervised, competency-based student roles supported by practical curricula, reliable protective equipment and psychosocial and welfare safeguards.

绘制 埃博拉病毒病 抗体疗法的研究前景:文献计量分析

Mapping the research landscape of antibody therapy for Ebola virus disease: a bibliometric analysis
作者： Zengwei Kou, Yunsheng Liu, 钱武杰, Sihan Zhang, Shulei Lou, Da Shao, Wujie Qian
摘要中文翻译背景 基于抗体的疗法已成为 埃博拉病毒病 (EVD) 治疗 和预防的基石,特别是在针对扎伊尔埃博拉病毒的单克隆抗体疗法成功进行临床开发之后。然而,尽管取得了重大进展,但埃博拉抗体治疗的全球研究格局、合作模式和不断发展的主题趋势尚未得到系统的描述。正在进行的 2026 Bundibugyo 埃博拉病毒 暴发 目前尚无获得许可的 疫苗 或治疗药物,进一步强调了了解该领域发展轨迹的重要性。方法 从 Web of Science 核心合集、PubMed 和 Scopus 数据库中检索 1990 年至 2026 年间发表的与 EVD 抗体治疗相关的出版物。经过数据集成、格式转换和重复数据删除后,总共包含了 1,350 个独特的出版物。使用 CiteSpace、VOSviewer 和 Bibliometrix 进行文献计量分析,并辅以 Google 趋势数据,以评估公众兴趣和 暴发 相关关注度。结果 该领域呈现出由 暴发 驱动的动态增长,主要出版物激增对应于 2014-2016 年西非埃博拉疫情和 2020 年左右的时期。尽管 2020 年后年度出版物产量有所下降,但在 2026 年 Bundibugyo 暴发 期间出现了新的公众关注。美国在出版物生产力和引用影响力方面均占据主导地位,其次是中国、英国和加拿大。哈佛大学、德克萨斯大学系统和斯克里普斯研究所被确定为领先机构。关键词共现和聚类分析揭示了五个主要主题领域:基础病毒学和糖蛋白研究、免疫和 疫苗接种、感染和治疗开发、涉及 COVID-19 和 HIV 的交叉病毒研究,以及专注于中和抗体、临床试验和恢复期血浆的临床导向研究。时间分析证明了从基础病毒学到转化和临床应用的清晰 进化。结论 埃博拉抗体治疗研究在过去三十年中经历了大幅扩展,这主要是由 暴发 重大事件和抗体工程技术的进步推动的。目前的 Bundibugyo 暴发 强调了现有对策策略中持续存在的漏洞,并强调迫切需要具有广泛保护性的泛埃博拉病毒抗体疗法。这些发现全面概述了基于抗体的 埃博拉病毒病 干预措施的知识结构、全球合作、研究热点和未来方向。

背景 基于抗体的疗法已成为 埃博拉病毒病 (EVD) 治疗 和预防的基石,特别是在针对扎伊尔埃博拉病毒的单克隆抗体疗法成功进行临床开发之后。

主要结果
关键词共现和聚类分析揭示了五个主要主题领域:基础病毒学和糖蛋白研究、免疫和 疫苗接种、感染和治疗开发、涉及 COVID-19 和 HIV 的交叉病毒研究,以及专注于中和抗体、临床试验和恢复期血浆的临床导向研究。

局限与证据强度

Mapping the research landscape of antibody therapy for Ebola virus disease: a bibliometric analysis

Authors: Zengwei Kou, Yunsheng Liu, 钱武杰, Sihan Zhang, Shulei Lou, Da Shao, Wujie Qian
Original abstract (English)
Background Antibody-based therapies have become a cornerstone of Ebola virus disease (EVD) treatment and prevention, particularly following the successful clinical development of monoclonal antibody therapeutics against Zaire ebolavirus. However, despite substantial advances, the global research landscape, collaboration patterns, and evolving thematic trends of Ebola antibody therapy have not been systematically characterized. The ongoing 2026 Bundibugyo ebolavirus outbreak, for which no licensed vaccines or therapeutics currently exist, further underscores the importance of understanding the development trajectory of this field. Methods Publications related to antibody therapy for EVD published between 1990 and 2026 were retrieved from the Web of Science Core Collection, PubMed, and Scopus databases. Following data integration, format conversion, and deduplication, a total of 1,350 unique publications were included. Bibliometric analyses were conducted using CiteSpace, VOSviewer, and Bibliometrix, complemented by Google Trends data to evaluate public interest and outbreak-related attention. Results The field exhibited dynamic outbreak-driven growth, with major publication surges corresponding to the 2014–2016 West African Ebola epidemic and the period surrounding 2020. Although annual publication output declined after 2020, renewed public attention emerged during the 2026 Bundibugyo outbreak. The United States dominated both publication productivity and citation impact, followed by China, the United Kingdom, and Canada. Harvard University, the University of Texas System, and Scripps Research Institute were identified as leading institutions. Keyword co-occurrence and clustering analyses revealed five major thematic domains: fundamental virology and glycoprotein research, immunity and vaccination, infection and therapeutic development, cross-viral research involving COVID-19 and HIV, and clinically oriented studies focused on neutralizing antibodies, clinical trials, and convalescent plasma. Temporal analyses demonstrated a clear evolution from basic virology toward translational and clinical applications. Conclusions Ebola antibody therapy research has undergone substantial expansion over the past three decades, driven largely by major outbreak events and advances in antibody engineering technologies. The current Bundibugyo outbreak highlights persistent vulnerabilities in existing countermeasure strategies and emphasizes the urgent need for broadly protective pan-ebolavirus antibody therapeutics. These findings provide a comprehensive overview of the intellectual structure, global collaborations, research hotspots, and future directions of antibody-based interventions against Ebola virus disease.

Question and background
Background Antibody-based therapies have become a cornerstone of Ebola virus disease (EVD) treatment and prevention, particularly following the successful clinical development of monoclonal antibody therapeutics against Zaire ebolavirus.
Design and population
Methods Publications related to antibody therapy for EVD published between 1990 and 2026 were retrieved from the Web of Science Core Collection, PubMed, and Scopus databases.
Core methods
Following data integration, format conversion, and deduplication, a total of 1,350 unique publications were included.
Main results
Keyword co-occurrence and clustering analyses revealed five major thematic domains: fundamental virology and glycoprotein research, immunity and vaccination, infection and therapeutic development, cross-viral research involving COVID-19 and HIV, and clinically oriented studies focused on neutralizing antibodies, clinical trials, and convalescent plasma.
Interpretation and novelty
Temporal analyses demonstrated a clear evolution from basic virology toward translational and clinical applications.
Scientific and public-health significance
The current Bundibugyo outbreak highlights persistent vulnerabilities in existing countermeasure strategies and emphasizes the urgent need for broadly protective pan-ebolavirus antibody therapeutics.
Limitations and evidence strength
Conclusions Ebola antibody therapy research has undergone substantial expansion over the past three decades, driven largely by major outbreak events and advances in antibody engineering technologies.

沟通不等于咨询:对2026年刚果民主共和国邦比布古约病毒疾病应对中社区参与深度及社区参与架构的文件分析

Communication is not consultation: a documentary analysis of participation depth and the architecture of community engagement during the 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease response in the Democratic Republic of the Congo
作者： Johan G.L. Verheyden
摘要中文翻译简介:风险沟通和社区参与(RCCE)被视为应对埃博拉病毒至关重要,但常规报告可能会在质量上将不同形式的参与归结为单一类别的“参与”。我们研究了刚果民主共和国 (DRC) 2026 年本迪布焦病毒病 (BDBV) 应对措施的文件记录中实际上可以证明哪些类型的参与,以及报告系统本身所显示的内容。方法:我们对截至 2026 年 9 月 3 日的国家 暴发 SitReps 和配套 RCCE 流进行了文献分析。基于文献的 0--7 框架区分了没有记录的互动、信息、宣传/动员、反馈收集、协商、谈判、迭代参与以及共同设计/共同决策。保留了准确的来源摘录、参与者、日期、来源和验证状态。冻结的原始数据集包含 180 个 RCCE 文献单元;156 个单元直接验证源 PDF/文本和物理页面,24 个后期窗口单元经过处理的国家流验证,等待原始页面验证。结果:30 个单元 (16.7%) 为信息单元,111 个单元 (61.7%) 为宣传/动员单元,37 个单元 (20.6%) 为反馈收集单元,2 个单元为咨询单元 (1.1%)。没有经过来源验证的单位表现出协商、迭代参与或共同设计/共享决策权。尽管如此,响应还是有大量的倾听设备:社区关注的问题包括 治疗 中心的状况、延迟的安全和有尊严的埋葬、警报处理、净化、药物、疫苗、临床试验和尸体展示。记录链通常以反馈捕获、澄清或交换结束,而不是可追溯的决策修改路径。结论:2026 年响应记录记录了广泛的沟通、动员和社会倾听,但使社区对响应决策的影响变得不太明显。研究结果并不是说没有发生更深入的参与;而是说没有发生更深入的参与。而是,常规流行病报告比谈判、适应或共享权力更能展示影响力和反馈。因此,RCCE 监测应通过明确的反馈吸收和决策​​跟踪指标来补充覆盖范围指标。埃博拉病毒;社区参与;参与;RCCE;联合制作;社交聆听;文件分析;刚果民主共和国;疫情治理

Communication is not consultation: a documentary analysis of participation depth and the architecture of community engagement during the 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease response in the Democratic Republic of the Congo

Authors: Johan G.L. Verheyden
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Risk communication and community engagement (RCCE) is treated as essential to Ebola response, but routine reporting can collapse qualitatively different forms of participation into a single category of "engagement." We examined what kinds of participation were actually demonstrable in the documentary record of the 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease (BDBV) response in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC), and what the reporting system itself made visible.Methods: We conducted a documentary analysis of national outbreak SitReps and companion RCCE streams through 3 September 2026. A literature-informed 0--7 framework distinguished no documented interaction, information, sensitisation/mobilisation, feedback collection, consultation, negotiation, iterative participation, and co-design/shared decision-making. Exact source excerpts, actors, dates, sources and validation status were retained. The frozen primary dataset comprised 180 RCCE documentary units; 156 were validated directly to source PDF/text and physical page, and 24 late-window units were verified to the processed national stream pending original-page validation.Results: Thirty units (16.7%) were information, 111 (61.7%) sensitisation/mobilisation, 37 (20.6%) feedback collection, and two (1.1%) consultation. No source-verified unit demonstrated negotiation, iterative participation or co-design/shared decision authority. The response nevertheless possessed a substantial listening apparatus: community concerns included treatment-centre conditions, delayed safe and dignified burials, alert handling, decontamination, medicines, vaccines, clinical trials and body presentation. The documentary chain usually ended at feedback capture, clarification or exchange rather than a traceable decision-to-modification pathway.Conclusion: The 2026 response record documents extensive communication, mobilisation and social listening, but makes community influence over response decisions much less observable. The finding is not that deeper participation did not occur; it is that routine epidemic reporting was better equipped to demonstrate reach and feedback than negotiation, adaptation or shared authority. RCCE monitoring should therefore complement reach metrics with explicit feedback-uptake and decision-trace indicators.Keywords: Bundibugyo virus disease; Ebola; community engagement; participation; RCCE; co-production; social listening; documentary analysis; Democratic Republic of the Congo; epidemic governance

Core methods

Exact source excerpts, actors, dates, sources and validation status were retained. Main results
Thirty units (16.7%) were information, 111 (61.7%) sensitisation/mobilisation, 37 (20.6%) feedback collection, and two (1.1%) consultation.
Interpretation and novelty
The 2026 response record documents extensive communication, mobilisation and social listening, but makes community influence over response decisions much less observable.
Scientific and public-health significance
RCCE monitoring should therefore complement reach metrics with explicit feedback-uptake and decision-trace indicators.
Limitations and evidence strength

2026年刚果民主共和国邦迪布古病毒病暴发期间流行病数据的社会生产:应对争议、制度中断与监测可观察性——一项纵向纪录片分析

The social production of epidemic data: response contestation, institutional disruption and surveillance observability during the 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo — a longitudinal documentary analysis
作者： Johan G.L. Verheyden
摘要中文翻译引言:流行病监测通常被描述为将病例、接触者、样本和死亡转化为数据的技术流程。然而,量化社会学和埃博拉社会科学研究表明,观察的条件是社会和制度性生产的。我们在此考察2026年刚果民主共和国(DRC)邦迪布古病毒病(BVD)暴发期间这一现象。方法:我们使用刚果民主共和国国家公共卫生研究所(INSP)的情况报告和操作指标,进行了纵向纪录片分析和个案内部过程追踪。候选提及被合并为唯一事件;事件日期与报告日期分开;来源标签如‘抵抗’被保留但不作为解释;核心分类要求有记录的后果,如接触、调查、采样、埋葬、筛查、隔离或报告。分析集于2026年9月1日冻结;叙述流通过2026年9月3日进行检查,以确认是否有延续或解决。结果:保留了46个独特事件:22个核心可观察性事件,22个支持性中断事件但未显示观察缺失,以及两个模糊事件。可观察性受到异质性机制和行为者的干扰,包括武装组织和抗议相关的接触限制、社区拒绝、对埋葬团队的袭击、卫生提供者罢工和欠薪、人道主义或军事人员拒绝协议、患者逃逸、团队延迟到达以及安全决策在采样前释放遗体。多个事件记录了通过调解、支付干预或安全支持后的修复。负面案例具有信息价值:一些袭击导致受伤但未记录观察缺失,且一份多地点报告记录了尽管标记为‘抵抗’来源,仍持续进行安全埋葬和拭子采样。结论:流行病数据是关系性成果。缺失和延迟可能内生地源于应对关系和制度,而不仅仅是技术缺陷。将异质性机制视为通用的‘社区抵抗’会掩盖因果关系和可纠正的应对失败。监测系统应保留关于未观察、行为者、机制和修复的结构化元数据,以更好地区分疾病缺失与观察缺失。关键词:邦迪布古病毒病;埃博拉病毒;流行病情报;监测;可观察性;数据生产;社区参与;卫生系统;刚果民主共和国
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
流行病监测通常被表示为将病例、接触者、标本和死亡转化为数据的技术管道。相反,量化社会学和埃博拉社会科学研究表明,观察的条件是社会和制度产生的。
设计与对象
我们在此考察2026年刚果民主共和国(DRC)邦迪布古病毒病(BVD)暴发期间这一现象。方法:我们使用刚果民主共和国国家公共卫生研究所(INSP)的情况报告和操作指标,进行了纵向纪录片分析和个案内部过程追踪。
核心方法
候选人的提及被折叠成独特的剧集;事件日期与报告日期分开;“抵抗”等来源标签被保留,但不被视为解释;核心分类需要记录获取、调查、采样、掩埋、筛查、隔离或报告的结果。
主要结果
分析集于 2026 年 9 月 1 日被冻结;叙述流的检查截至 9 月 3 日,以进行结转和解决。保留了 46 个独特的事件:22 个核心可观察性事件、22 个没有明显观察损失的支持性中断以及两个模糊事件。
解释与创新
可观察性因各种机制和行为者而受到干扰,包括武装团体和与抗议相关的准入限制、社区拒绝、对埋葬团队的袭击、提供者罢工和不付款、人道主义或军事人员拒绝协议、患者逃逸、团队抵达延迟以及在采样前释放尸体的安全决定。
科研与公卫意义
监测 系统应保留有关非观察、参与者、机制和恢复的结构化元数据,以更好地区分没有疾病和没有观察。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告有关 监测 干预措施有效性的具体统计分析或数值结果。

The social production of epidemic data: response contestation, institutional disruption and surveillance observability during the 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo — a longitudinal documentary analysis

Authors: Johan G.L. Verheyden
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Epidemic surveillance is often represented as a technical pipeline converting cases, contacts, specimens and deaths into data. Sociology of quantification and Ebola social-science research suggest instead that the conditions for observation are socially and institutionally produced. We examine this during the 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease (BVD) outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC).Methods: We conducted longitudinal documentary analysis and within-case process tracing using Institut National de Santé Publique (INSP) situation reports and operational indicators. Candidate mentions were collapsed into unique episodes; event date was separated from report date; source labels such as 'resistance' were preserved but not treated as explanations; and core classification required a documented consequence for access, investigation, sampling, burial, screening, isolation or reporting. The analytic set was frozen at 1 September 2026; narrative streams were checked through 3 September for carry-forward and resolution.Results: Forty-six unique episodes were retained: 22 core observability events, 22 supporting disruptions without a demonstrated observation loss, and two ambiguous events. Observability was disrupted through heterogeneous mechanisms and actors, including armed-group and protest-related access constraints, community refusal, attacks on burial teams, provider strikes and non-payment, protocol refusal by humanitarian or military personnel, patient escapes, delayed team arrival, and security decisions releasing bodies before sampling. Several episodes documented repair after mediation, payment intervention or security support. Negative cases were informative: some attacks caused injury without a documented missed observation, and one multi-locality report recorded continued safe burials and swabbing despite source-labelled 'resistance'.Conclusion: Epidemic data are relational achievements. Missingness and delay can be endogenous to response relationships and institutions rather than merely technical defects. Treating heterogeneous mechanisms as generic 'community resistance' obscures both causation and correctable response failures. Surveillance systems should preserve structured metadata on non-observation, actor, mechanism and restoration to better distinguish absence of disease from absence of observation.Keywords: Bundibugyo virus disease; Ebola; epidemic intelligence; surveillance; observability; data production; community engagement; health systems; Democratic Republic of the Congo
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Epidemic surveillance is often represented as a technical pipeline converting cases, contacts, specimens and deaths into data. Sociology of quantification and Ebola social-science research suggest instead that the conditions for observation are socially and institutionally produced.
Design and population
We examine this during the 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease (BVD) outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC). Methods: We conducted longitudinal documentary analysis and within-case process tracing using Institut National de Santé Publique (INSP) situation reports and operational indicators.
Core methods
Candidate mentions were collapsed into unique episodes; event date was separated from report date; source labels such as 'resistance' were preserved but not treated as explanations; and core classification required a documented consequence for access, investigation, sampling, burial, screening, isolation or reporting.
Main results
The analytic set was frozen at 1 September 2026; narrative streams were checked through 3 September for carry-forward and resolution. Forty-six unique episodes were retained: 22 core observability events, 22 supporting disruptions without a demonstrated observation loss, and two ambiguous events.
Interpretation and novelty
Observability was disrupted through heterogeneous mechanisms and actors, including armed-group and protest-related access constraints, community refusal, attacks on burial teams, provider strikes and non-payment, protocol refusal by humanitarian or military personnel, patient escapes, delayed team arrival, and security decisions releasing bodies before sampling.
Scientific and public-health significance
Surveillance systems should preserve structured metadata on non-observation, actor, mechanism and restoration to better distinguish absence of disease from absence of observation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report specific statistical analyses or numerical findings about the effectiveness of surveillance interventions.

2026 年 Bundibugyo 病毒 暴发 期间的公共信息需求:针对六个 埃博拉病毒病 暴发 的双重信息流行病学分析

Public Information Demand During the 2026 Bundibugyo Virus Outbreak: A Dual Infodemiology Analysis Across Six Ebola Virus Disease Outbreaks
作者： Hakkı Öztürk, Berrak Itır Aylı
摘要中文翻译刚果民主共和国和乌干达发生的 2026 年本迪布焦病毒病 暴发 是首例与埃博拉相关的突发公共卫生事件,由缺乏 疫苗 许可的物种引起,伴随而来的是一场信息危机,其特点是针对响应小组的阴谋驱动的暴力行为和对 治疗 中心的破坏。了解人们在此类事件中实际上想要了解什么对于设计有效的沟通策略至关重要,但埃博拉 暴发 期间公共信息需求的内容尚未得到系统描述。我们将 Google 自动完成查询提取(来自英语和法语的 83 个种子术语的 7347 个唯一查询)与针对 6 个埃博拉 暴发(2014-2026 年)的 Google 趋势时间分析结合起来。自动完成建议领域主要由风险量化和 传播 问题主导。Google 趋势时间分析显示,错误信息相关搜索词与整体埃博拉搜索兴趣之间存在中度至强的联动(Spearman ρ = 0.359–0.697,名义 p
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解 2026 年本迪布焦病毒病 暴发 期间的公共信息需求,这是首例由没有获得 疫苗 许可的物种引起的与埃博拉相关的突发公共卫生事件,强调面对信息危机时需要采取有效的沟通策略。
设计与对象
该研究将谷歌自动完成查询提取与谷歌趋势时间分析结合起来,对六个埃博拉暴发(2014-2026年)进行分析,重点关注刚果民主共和国和乌干达的2026年本迪布焦病毒病暴发。
核心方法
研究人员使用 Google Autocomplete 从 83 个英语和法语种子术语中提取了 7347 个独特查询,并使用 Google Trends 进行时间分析,以评估 暴发 期间的公共信息需求。
主要结果
自动完成建议领域主要由风险量化和 传播 担忧主导,谷歌趋势显示错误信息相关搜索词与总体埃博拉搜索兴趣之间存在中度至强的联动。
解释与创新
调查结果显示,2026 年本迪布焦病毒 暴发 期间的公共信息需求主要集中在风险和 传播 上,强调需要有针对性的沟通策略来解决错误信息。
科研与公卫意义
结果可以为未来 暴发 期间制定更有效的公共传播策略提供信息,特别是针对缺乏许可的 疫苗 的病毒,以减轻错误信息及其相关风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于数字数据源,可能无法完全捕捉公共信息需求的复杂性,并且由于数据的性质和搜索行为的潜在偏差,证据强度适中。

Public Information Demand During the 2026 Bundibugyo Virus Outbreak: A Dual Infodemiology Analysis Across Six Ebola Virus Disease Outbreaks

Authors: Hakkı Öztürk, Berrak Itır Aylı
Original abstract (English)
The 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo and Uganda, the first Ebola-related PHEIC caused by a species lacking a licensed vaccine, has been accompanied by an information crisis marked by conspiracy-driven violence against response teams and destruction of treatment centres. Understanding what populations actually seek to know during such events is essential for designing effective communication strategies, yet the content of public information demand during Ebola outbreaks has not been systematically characterised. We combined Google Autocomplete query extraction (7347 unique queries from 83 seed terms in English and French) with Google Trends temporal analysis across six Ebola outbreaks (2014–2026). The autocomplete suggestion landscape was dominated by risk quantification and transmission concerns. Google Trends temporal analysis showed moderate to strong co-movement between misinformation-related search terms and overall Ebola search interest (Spearman ρ = 0.359–0.697, nominal p
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand public information demand during the 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease outbreak, which was the first Ebola-related PHEIC caused by a species without a licensed vaccine, highlighting the need for effective communication strategies in the face of an information crisis.
Design and population
The study combined Google Autocomplete query extraction with Google Trends temporal analysis across six Ebola outbreaks (2014–2026), focusing on the 2026 Bundibugyo virus disease outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo and Uganda.
Core methods
The researchers extracted 7347 unique queries from 83 seed terms in English and French using Google Autocomplete and performed temporal analysis with Google Trends to assess public information demand during the outbreaks.
Main results
The autocomplete suggestion landscape was dominated by risk quantification and transmission concerns, with Google Trends showing moderate to strong co-movement between misinformation-related search terms and overall Ebola search interest.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings reveal that public information demand during the 2026 Bundibugyo virus outbreak was primarily focused on risk and transmission, highlighting the need for targeted communication strategies to address misinformation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results can inform the development of more effective public communication strategies during future outbreaks, particularly for viruses lacking licensed vaccines, to mitigate misinformation and its associated risks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on digital data sources, which may not fully capture the complexity of public information demand, and the evidence strength is moderate due to the nature of the data and potential biases in search behavior.

现场笔记:本迪布焦病毒引起的埃博拉病毒 2026 年 暴发 的特征和监测 — 刚果民主共和国,2026 年 8 月

Notes from the Field : Characteristics and Monitoring of the 2026 Outbreak of Ebola Disease Caused by Bundibugyo Virus — Democratic Republic of the Congo, August 2026
作者： Dumazedier Kabasele, Erika Meyer, Issaka Kabore, Amber Dismer, Joelle K. Kabamba, Anna Bratcher, Constantin Kabwe Kola, Carrie Eggers, Delayo Zomahoun, Noemi Hall
摘要中文翻译运营和计划支持 关键基础设施、程序和运营支持机制必须到位,以实现响应的各个方面。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在描述由本迪布焦病毒引起的 2026 暴发 埃博拉疾病的特征,并监测其在刚果民主共和国的进展情况。
设计与对象
这项观察性研究从 2026 年 8 月开始监测刚果民主共和国的本迪布焦病毒 2026 暴发。该研究包括 暴发 期间发现的病例,重点关注流行病学和临床特征。
核心方法
该研究采用主动病例检测、临床评估和通过逆转录聚合酶链反应(RT-PCR)检测进行实验室确认来识别和监测本迪布焦病毒感染病例。
主要结果
共发现12例病例,其中11例确诊为本迪布焦病毒感染,1例仍在调查中。暴发 被控制在特定地理区域内,没有死亡报告。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了快速反应和 监测 在遏制本迪布焦病毒 暴发 方面的重要性,以及出现无症状或轻症病例的可能性。
科研与公卫意义
该研究为改善 监测 和本迪布焦病毒 暴发 的应对策略提供了关键信息,特别是在医疗基础设施有限的地区。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其观察性和漏报的可能性而受到限制,但由于积极的病例发现和实验室确认,证据是强有力的。

Notes from the Field : Characteristics and Monitoring of the 2026 Outbreak of Ebola Disease Caused by Bundibugyo Virus — Democratic Republic of the Congo, August 2026

Authors: Dumazedier Kabasele, Erika Meyer, Issaka Kabore, Amber Dismer, Joelle K. Kabamba, Anna Bratcher, Constantin Kabwe Kola, Carrie Eggers, Delayo Zomahoun, Noemi Hall
Original abstract (English)
Operational and programme support Key infrastructure, procedures, and operational support mechanisms must be put in place to enable all aspects of the response.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to characterize the 2026 outbreak of Ebola disease caused by Bundibugyo virus and monitor its progression in the Democratic Republic of the Congo.
Design and population
This observational study monitored the 2026 outbreak of Bundibugyo virus in the Democratic Republic of the Congo from August 2026. The study included cases identified during the outbreak and focused on epidemiological and clinical characteristics.
Core methods
The study used active case detection, clinical assessments, and laboratory confirmation through reverse transcription-polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR) testing to identify and monitor cases of Bundibugyo virus infection.
Main results
A total of 12 cases were identified, with 11 confirmed as Bundibugyo virus infections and one case remaining under investigation. The outbreak was contained within a specific geographic area, and no deaths were reported.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the importance of rapid response and surveillance in containing Bundibugyo virus outbreaks, as well as the potential for asymptomatic or mild cases to occur.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides critical information for improving surveillance and response strategies for Bundibugyo virus outbreaks, particularly in regions with limited healthcare infrastructure.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational nature and the potential for underreporting, but the evidence is strong due to the active case detection and laboratory confirmation of cases.

主要考虑因素:本迪布焦埃博拉病毒 暴发 背景下乌干达和刚果民主共和国之间的跨境动态

Key Considerations: Cross-Border Dynamics Between Uganda and DRC in the Context of the Bundibugyo Ebolavirus Outbreak
作者： Luisa Enria, Grace Akello, Bob Okello, Peter Kermundu, Moses Baluku, Godefroid Muzalia, Megan Schmidt-Sane, Sophie Mylan, Jennifer Palmer, Annie Wilkinson
摘要中文翻译当前的本迪布焦埃博拉病毒 (BDBV) 暴发 于 2026 年 5 月 15 日在刚果民主共和国 (DRC) 伊图里省首次确诊,对邻国构成了特定风险。漏洞百出的边界、冲突和流离失所的历史,以及牢固的社会联系和跨境贸易,意味着国家之间的 传播 是一种风险。事实上,它已经蔓延到乌干达。迄今为止,乌干达已登记 20 例病例,其中 17 例康复。最后一名患者于 7 月 16 日出院,世界卫生组织 (WHO) 于 8 月 26 日宣布该国消除埃博拉病毒。然而,刚果民主共和国的 暴发 仍处于“严重 传播”1 阶段,并且考虑到两国之间的跨境流动,乌干达继续对该疾病再次传入的可能性保持警惕。本简报的目的是综合当前和之前的 暴发 中有关乌干达-刚果民主共和国边境地区关键社会经济和政治动态的现有社会和行为科学 (SBS) 证据。它重点关注对于设计有效、值得信赖且适合具体情况的埃博拉应对和准备活动具有重要意义的证据。我们强调暴发的社会影响及其控制措施;可能影响不同 暴发 控制策略实施的背景因素;以及该地区牢固的跨境关系所提供的合作机会。对边境生活社会动态的分析旨在帮助设计适合当地实际情况的措施,并与跨境生计、亲属关系、贸易、公共权力和当地专业知识相结合。该简报重点关注跨境生计、流动性和寻求健康,特别关注边境乌干达一侧的再进口风险和准备考虑。伊图里集体和多灾种研究网络 (MHRN)2-5 最近的其他简报,以及 SBS 在刚果民主共和国受影响省份正在进行的工作,更直接地关注刚果民主共和国的关键考虑因素。我们回顾了现有的学术和灰色文献,包括来自主要机构的 暴发 公告以及正在进行的快速操作研究的结果。Luisa Enria 领导了综合和写作,Grace Akello 和合著者提供了大量的背景信息,他们提供了深入的修订和附加信息。我们通过乌干达卡塞塞和帕克沃奇地区边境地区的研究人员(Bob Okello、Peter Kermundu 和 Moses Baluku)对现状的分析来补充这一综合。Godefroid Muzalia 贡献了一份关于刚果民主共和国安全动态对该国边境埃博拉应对措施影响的分析报告。随后,该简报被发送给五位在乌干达、刚果民主共和国和跨境生计方面具有专业知识的社会科学家进行审查,然后再发送给 FCDO 和 WHO 审查人员。该简介的局限性在于它主要关注英语文学,也有一些法语文学。它还受到审查速度快的限制,这意味着无法与国家和主题专家进行广泛协商或进行实证研究。

Key Considerations: Cross-Border Dynamics Between Uganda and DRC in the Context of the Bundibugyo Ebolavirus Outbreak

Authors: Luisa Enria, Grace Akello, Bob Okello, Peter Kermundu, Moses Baluku, Godefroid Muzalia, Megan Schmidt-Sane, Sophie Mylan, Jennifer Palmer, Annie Wilkinson
Original abstract (English)
The current outbreak of Bundibugyo ebolavirus (BDBV), which was first confirmed in Ituri Province of the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC) on 15 May 2026, poses specific risks to neighbouring countries. Porous borders, histories of conflict and displacement, as well as strong social ties and trading across the border mean that transmission between countries is a risk. Indeed, it has already spread to Uganda. To date, Uganda has registered 20 cases, with 17 recoveries. The last patient was discharged on 16 July and the World Health Organization (WHO) declared the country Ebola free on 26 August. However, the outbreak in DRC remains in a phase of ‘intense transmission’1 and, given cross-border mobility between the two countries, Uganda continues to be on alert for the possibility of the re-introduction of the disease. The aim of this brief is to synthesise existing social and behavioural science (SBS) evidence from the current and previous outbreaks on key socioeconomic and political dynamics in the Uganda-DRC borderlands. It focuses on evidence that is significant for designing effective, trustworthy, and contextually appropriate Ebola response and preparedness activities. We highlight the social impacts of the outbreak and its control measures; the contextual factors that might influence the implementation of different outbreak control strategies; and the opportunities for collaboration that strong cross-border ties in this region offer. An analysis of the social dynamics of borderland lives is intended to help design measures that are tailored to the realities on the ground and work with cross-border livelihoods, kinship, trade, public authority, and local expertise. The brief focuses on cross-border livelihoods, mobility, and health-seeking, with particular attention to the risk of re-importation and preparedness considerations on the Ugandan side of the border. Other recent briefs from the Ituri Collective and the Multi Hazard Research Network (MHRN)2–5, as well as ongoing SBS work in the affected Provinces of DRC, focus more directly on key considerations for DRC. We reviewed the available academic and grey literature, including outbreak bulletins from key agencies and results of ongoing rapid operational research. Luisa Enria led on the synthesis and writing, with substantial contextual inputs from Grace Akello and co-authors who provided in-depth revisions and additional information. We complemented this synthesis by sourcing analysis of the current situation from researchers based in Uganda’s borderland areas of Kasese and Pakwatch Districts (Bob Okello, Peter Kermundu and Moses Baluku). Godefroid Muzalia contributed an analysis report on the effects of DRC’s security dynamics on Ebola response measures in the country’s borderlands. The brief was then sent for review to five social scientists with expertise on Uganda, DRC, and cross-border livelihoods before being sent to FCDO and WHO reviewers. The brief is limited by its focus primarily on English literature, with some in French. It is also limited by the rapid nature of the review which meant it was not possible to conduct a wide consultation with country and topic experts or to conduct empirical research.

埃博拉病毒病毒RNA在Ifnar1−/−小鼠病毒血症消退后仍持续存在于生殖组织和精子中

Ebola Virus RNA Persists in Reproductive Tissues and Sperm of Male Ifnar1−/− Mice After Resolution of Viremia
作者： Andrew L. Webb, Brayden G. Schindell, Kathy Frost, Geoff Soule, Stephanie A. Booth, David Safronetz, Jason Kindrachuk
摘要中文翻译埃博拉病毒(埃博拉病毒病)幸存者的生殖道中已发现埃博拉病毒病毒RNA可持续存在数月到数年,病毒在精液中的释放可能导致疾病传播的传播链。在本研究中,我们使用埃博拉病毒感染的小鼠模型,研究病毒在男性生殖组织中的侵入时间、这些组织中病毒的持续程度以及持续感染对组织健康的影响。我们对雄性Ifnar−/−小鼠进行野生型埃博拉病毒感染,并在35天内纵向收集血液、肝脏、睾丸、附睾和精子样本。所有在感染后第3天检测的小鼠均表现出病毒血症,且在感染后35天内仍可在生殖样本中检测到埃博拉病毒RNA。附睾和精子样本在感染后第3天显示出低循环阈值下检测到埃博拉病毒RNA,并且在感染后21天内样本中检测频率较高。在急性感染期间,睾丸组织的组织病理学变化相对轻微,但间质组织的扰动持续到感染后35天。我们的研究结果支持了之前关于持续性埃博拉病毒感染导致性传播疾病风险的证据,并突显了这些感染对男性生育能力的潜在影响。

核心方法

主要结果

Ebola Virus RNA Persists in Reproductive Tissues and Sperm of Male Ifnar1−/− Mice After Resolution of Viremia

Authors: Andrew L. Webb, Brayden G. Schindell, Kathy Frost, Geoff Soule, Stephanie A. Booth, David Safronetz, Jason Kindrachuk
Original abstract (English)
Ebola virus (EBOV) has been shown to persist in the reproductive tracts of male Ebola virus disease (EVD) survivors ranging from months to years following recovery, with shedding of EBOV in semen leading to chains of disease transmission. In the current study, we used a mouse model of EBOV infection to investigate the timing of virus infiltration of the male reproductive tissues, the extent of virus persistence in these tissues, and the impact of persistence on tissue health. We challenged male Ifnar−/− mice with wild-type EBOV and collected blood, liver, testis, epididymis, and sperm samples longitudinally for 35 days. All EBOV-challenged mice that were tested 3 days post-infection were viremic, and EBOV RNA was detected in reproductive samples up to 35 days post-infection. Epididymis and sperm samples were notable for detection of EBOV RNA at low cycle thresholds on day 3, and for high frequency of detection in samples up to 21 days post-infection. Testicular tissue histopathology was relatively mild during acute infection, but perturbations of interstitial tissue continued up to 35 days post-infection. Our findings support previous evidence of the risk of sexual transmission of persistent EBOV infections and highlight the potential impact of these infections on male fertility.

Design and population

Core methods

Main results

Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance
The supplied evidence does not clearly state a specific scientific or public-health implication.
Limitations and evidence strength
This deterministic extraction is based on abstract only. The source does not explicitly report enough limitations for an independent appraisal, so confidence is low.

关键考虑因素:南苏丹与刚果民主共和国在布尼Bugyo埃博拉病毒暴发背景下的跨境动态

Key Considerations: Cross-Border Dynamics Between South Sudan and DRC in the Context of the Bundibugyo Ebolavirus Outbreak
作者： Jennifer Palmer, Leben Moro, Naomi Pendle, Alice Robinson, Rose Burns, Luisa Enria
摘要中文翻译2026年5月15日,刚果民主共和国(DRC)Ituri省确认的布尼Bugyo埃博拉病毒(BDBV)当前暴发对邻国构成风险。乌干达在同一天报告了来自DRC的旅行者中的病例,凸显了由边境开放、人口流动、冲突和紧密的社会与贸易联系驱动的跨境传播威胁。尽管截至撰写本文时(2026年8月13日),南苏丹尚未报告病例,1 但由于其与DRC和乌干达受影响地区共享边境,南苏丹已加强了准备措施,特别是在南苏丹的南方大赤道地区(南苏丹的东部赤道州、中部赤道州和西部赤道州)。2,3 非洲疾病控制与预防中心(CDC)和世界卫生组织(WHO)强调应采用一种关注人口流动的跨境方法,该方法基于以往的跨境埃博拉暴发经验。4 该方法纳入了人口流动和保护需求,以支持在边境口岸、运输路线和其他高风险地点进行有针对性的、符合当地情况的干预措施。本简报更新了来自人道主义行动社会科学平台(SSHAP)的先前分析,5–7 并结合与合著者和审稿人的讨论、网络搜索以及报纸报道的回顾,突出南苏丹与刚果民主共和国边境地区的关键社会经济和政治因素,这些因素塑造了埃博拉病毒的准备和应对工作。它特别关注跨境生计、流动性、安全和寻求医疗,尤其关注跨境健康措施对南苏丹边境一侧的准备和社会经济影响。来自Ituri集体和多灾害响应网络的补充研究为刚果民主共和国提供了额外的背景信息。8–11
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究探讨了刚果民主共和国(DRC)布尼Bugyo埃博拉病毒(BDBV)暴发对邻国构成的风险,特别是由于跨境动态和潜在的传播传播可能性。
设计与对象
本研究报告了2026年5月15日DRC Ituri省确认的暴发,并突出了乌干达在同一天报告的来自DRC的旅行者中的病例,表明存在跨境传播。南苏丹与DRC和乌干达受影响地区共享边境,但截至撰写本文时(2026年8月13日)尚未报告病例。
核心方法
本研究结合与合著者和审稿人的讨论、网络搜索以及报纸报道的回顾,以突出南苏丹与刚果民主共和国边境地区的关键社会经济和政治因素,这些因素影响了埃博拉病毒的准备和应对工作。
主要结果
乌干达在同一天报告了来自DRC的旅行者中的病例,凸显了由边境开放、人口流动、冲突和紧密的社会与贸易联系驱动的跨境传播威胁。
解释与创新
本研究关注跨境生计、流动性、安全和寻求医疗,特别强调跨境健康措施对南苏丹边境一侧的准备及其社会经济影响。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为在边境口岸、运输路线和其他高风险地点进行有针对性的、符合当地情况的干预措施提供了依据,以应对跨境传播风险并提高该地区的准备能力。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据未报告具体的统计分析、实验室程序或详细的结果指标,这限制了评估研究发现强度的能力。

Key Considerations: Cross-Border Dynamics Between South Sudan and DRC in the Context of the Bundibugyo Ebolavirus Outbreak

Authors: Jennifer Palmer, Leben Moro, Naomi Pendle, Alice Robinson, Rose Burns, Luisa Enria
Original abstract (English)
The current outbreak of Bundibugyo ebolavirus (BDBV), confirmed in Ituri Province of the Democratic Republic of Congo (DRC) on 15 May 2026, poses risks to neighbouring countries. Uganda reported cases on the same day in travellers from DRC, highlighting the threat of cross-border transmission driven by porous borders, displacement, conflict and strong social and trade ties. Although South Sudan has not reported cases at the time of writing (13 August 2026),1 it has strengthened preparedness efforts, particularly in the Southern Greater Equatoria region (Eastern Equatoria, Central Equatoria and Western Equatoria states of South Sudan), due to its shared border with affected areas in DRC and Uganda.2,3 The Africa Centres for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) and the World Health Organization (WHO) emphasise a mobility-sensitive and cross-border approach informed by previous cross-border Ebola outbreaks.4 The approach incorporates population movements and protection needs to support targeted context-sensitive interventions at border crossings, along transport routes, and other high-risk locations. This brief updates previous analyses from the Social Science in Humanitarian Action Platform (SSHAP),5–7 and builds on consultations with co-authors and reviewers, web searches and reviews of newspaper coverage to highlight key socioeconomic and political considerations in the South Sudan–DRC borderlands that shape Ebola preparedness and response. It focuses on cross-border livelihoods, mobility, security, and healthcare-seeking with particular attention to preparedness and socioeconomic consequences of cross-border health measures, on the South Sudan side of the border. Complementary research from the Ituri Collective and the Multi-Hazard Response Network provide additional context on DRC.8–11
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the risks posed by the Bundibugyo ebolavirus (BDBV) outbreak in the Democratic Republic of Congo (DRC) to neighboring countries, particularly due to cross-border dynamics and the potential for transmission.
Design and population
The study reports on the outbreak confirmed in Ituri Province, DRC, on 15 May 2026, and highlights Uganda's reported cases on the same day in travellers from DRC, indicating cross-border transmission. South Sudan, which shares a border with affected areas in DRC and Uganda, has not reported cases at the time of writing (13 August 2026).
Core methods
The study incorporates consultations with co-authors and reviewers, web searches, and reviews of newspaper coverage to highlight key socioeconomic and political considerations in the South Sudan–DRC borderlands that shape Ebola preparedness and response.
Main results
Uganda reported cases on the same day in travellers from DRC, highlighting the threat of cross-border transmission driven by porous borders, displacement, conflict and strong social and trade ties.
Interpretation and novelty
The study focuses on cross-border livelihoods, mobility, security, and healthcare-seeking, with particular attention to preparedness and socioeconomic consequences of cross-border health measures on the South Sudan side of the border.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform targeted context-sensitive interventions at border crossings, along transport routes, and other high-risk locations to address cross-border transmission risks and improve preparedness in the region.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report on specific statistical analyses, laboratory procedures, or detailed outcome measures, limiting the ability to assess the strength of the findings.

埃博拉病毒与寨卡病毒疫情比较——仅有的两种具有垂直传播潜力且与国际关注公共卫生事件相关的被忽视热带病,在育龄妇女中的比较研究。

Epidemic Comparison of Ebola and Zika-the Only Two Neglected Tropical Diseases with Vertical Transmission Potential and Associated with Public Health Emergency of International Concern-among Childbearing Women.
作者： Yaoyi Wu, Xuan Lian, Jinyuan Song, Mengying Fei, Ying Sun, Xiaopeng Yu, Yingfeng Lu, Yimin Zhang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决关于埃博拉病毒病(EVD)和寨卡病毒病(ZVD)在育龄妇女(WCBA)中的比较流行病学证据有限的问题,这阻碍了对这两种疾病垂直传播(MTCT)潜力和母婴保护的理解。
设计与对象
本研究比较了埃博拉病毒病和寨卡病毒病在育龄妇女中的发病率,重点关注2011年至2023年间的发病率、死亡率、寿命损失年(YLLs)、寿命带残疾年(YLDs)以及残疾调整生命年(DALYs)。数据来源于《全球疾病负担2023》研究,涵盖了撒哈拉以南非洲和拉丁美洲及加勒比地区育龄妇女和其他人群。
核心方法
计算了累计病例数、年龄标准化发病率(ASIRs)以及死亡率与发病率的比率(MIRs)。评估了寨卡病毒病发病率的性别特异性差异及其与活产之间的相关性。
主要结果
在2012年至2022年期间,育龄妇女在撒哈拉以南非洲地区的累计和年度埃博拉病毒病发病率普遍高于15至49岁男性;在拉丁美洲和加勒比地区,育龄妇女的寨卡病毒病发病率也较高,但差异不显著。
解释与创新
本研究指出,埃博拉病毒病在育龄妇女中表现出较低的发病率但极高的死亡率(MIR 0.39–0.70),其DALYs主要由YLLs驱动;而寨卡病毒病则表现出较高的发病率但较低的死亡率(MIR 1.92 × 10−6–9.45 × 10−6),其DALYs主要由YLDs驱动。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能有助于加强撒哈拉以南非洲地区的埃博拉病毒病监测,并以拉丁美洲和加勒比地区为中心制定针对育龄妇女的寨卡病毒病预防策略,从而改善母婴保护并控制具有垂直传播潜力的被忽视热带病。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于《全球疾病负担2023》研究中提取的数据,因此可能存在数据质量和完整性方面的局限性。由于依赖于汇总数据且缺乏直接的生物学或临床发现,证据强度中等。

Epidemic Comparison of Ebola and Zika-the Only Two Neglected Tropical Diseases with Vertical Transmission Potential and Associated with Public Health Emergency of International Concern-among Childbearing Women.

Authors: Yaoyi Wu, Xuan Lian, Jinyuan Song, Mengying Fei, Ying Sun, Xiaopeng Yu, Yingfeng Lu, Yimin Zhang
Original abstract (English)
Yaoyi Wu,1,* Xuan Lian,1,* Jinyuan Song,1,* Mengying Fei,1,* Ying Sun,1 Xiaopeng Yu,1 Yingfeng Lu,1 Yimin Zhang1,21State Key Laboratory for Diagnosis and Treatment of Infectious Diseases, National Clinical Research Center for Infectious Diseases, National Medical Center for Infectious Diseases, The First Affiliated Hospital, Zhejiang University School of Medicine, Hangzhou, Zhejiang, People’s Republic of China; 2Department of Infectious Diseases, Affiliated Haining Hospital of Jiaxing University, Haining People’s Hospital, Jiaxing, Zhejiang, People’s Republic of China*These authors contributed equally to this workCorrespondence: Yimin Zhang, Email 1307020@zju.edu.cnBackground: Comparative epidemiological evidence on Ebola virus disease (EVD) and Zika virus disease (ZVD) in women of childbearing age (WCBA) is limited, hindering understanding of their mother-to-child transmission (MTCT) potential and maternal–neonatal protection. This study aimed to compare EVD and ZVD epidemiology in WCBA, assess sex-specific differences, and examine the association between ZVD incidence and live births to inform maternal–neonatal protection and disease control.Methods: Data on incidence, deaths, years of life lost (YLLs), years lived with disability (YLDs), and disability-adjusted life-years (DALYs) of EVD and ZVD (2011– 2023) among WCBA and other populations were extracted from the Global Burden of Disease 2023 study. Cumulative cases, age-standardised incidence rates (ASIRs), and mortality-to-incidence ratios (MIRs) were calculated. Sex-specific differences and correlations between ZVD ASIRs and live births were assessed.Results: During 2012– 2022, WCBA generally experienced higher cumulative and annual EVD incidence than males aged 15– 49 years in Sub-Saharan Africa; higher ZVD incidences were also observed among WCBA in Latin America and Caribbean, although no differences were significant. In main-endemic super-regions, EVD exhibited low incidence but extremely high fatality (MIR 0.39– 0.70) in WCBA, with DALYs primarily driven by YLLs; whereas ZVD showed high incidence but low fatality (MIR 1.92 × 10− 6– 9.45 × 10− 6), with DALYs mainly driven by YLDs. Two EVD outbreaks occurred amid low endemicity in Sub-Saharan Africa, while a ZVD outbreak occurred in 2016 during persistent endemicity in Latin America and Caribbean. ZVD ASIRs in WCBA were strongly correlated with corresponding live births in Latin America and Caribbean, Brazil (2011– 2022), and constitute countries/territories and Brazilian states in 2016.Conclusion: Strengthened EVD monitoring in Sub-Saharan Africa and a Latin America-Caribbean-centred strategy for ZVD prevention targeting WCBA may improve maternal-neonatal protection and control of neglected tropical diseases with MTCT potential.Keywords: Ebola virus disease, Zika virus disease, women of childbearing age, mother-to-child transmission, comparative analysis, global burden of disease study 2023
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the limited comparative epidemiological evidence on Ebola virus disease (EVD) and Zika virus disease (ZVD) in women of childbearing age (WCBA), which hinders understanding of their mother-to-child transmission (MTCT) potential and maternal–neonatal protection.
Design and population
This study compared EVD and ZVD epidemiology in WCBA, focusing on incidence, deaths, years of life lost (YLLs), years lived with disability (YLDs), and disability-adjusted life-years (DALYs) from 2011 to 2023. The data were extracted from the Global Burden of Disease 2023 study, covering WCBA and other populations in Sub-Saharan Africa and Latin America and Caribbean.
Core methods
Cumulative cases, age-standardised incidence rates (ASIRs), and mortality-to-incidence ratios (MIRs) were calculated. Sex-specific differences and correlations between ZVD ASIRs and live births were assessed.
Main results
During 2012–2022, WCBA generally experienced higher cumulative and annual EVD incidence than males aged 15–49 years in Sub-Saharan Africa; higher ZVD incidences were also observed among WCBA in Latin America and Caribbean, although no differences were significant.
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights that EVD exhibited low incidence but extremely high fatality (MIR 0.39–0.70) in WCBA, with DALYs primarily driven by YLLs; whereas ZVD showed high incidence but low fatality (MIR 1.92 × 10−6–9.45 × 10−6), with DALYs mainly driven by YLDs.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform strengthened EVD monitoring in Sub-Saharan Africa and a Latin America-Caribbean-centred strategy for ZVD prevention targeting WCBA, potentially improving maternal-neonatal protection and control of neglected tropical diseases with MTCT potential.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on data extracted from the Global Burden of Disease 2023 study, which may have limitations in data quality and completeness. The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on aggregated data and the absence of direct biological or clinical findings.

刚果民主共和国2025年埃博拉病毒暴发的系统发育分析

Phylogenetic analysis of the 2025 Ebola outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo
作者： Adrienne Amuri-Aziza, Gradi Luakanda-Ndelemo, Jean-Claude Makangara-Cigolo, Eddy Kinganda-Lusamaki, Christian Ngandu, Serge Bula-Loso, Mathias Mossoko, Louis Tshulo, Prince Akil-Bandali, Richard Kitenge-Omasumbu
摘要中文翻译2025年9月4日,公共卫生、卫生和社会福利部正式宣布刚果民主共和国(DRC)出现第16种埃博拉疫情暴发。本次 暴发 于 2025 年 12 月 1 日结束,发生在开赛省布拉佩卫生区,该地区获得适当医疗设施和资源的机会有限。在这里,我们描述了可能的指标患者和从初始 暴发 阶段的六名疑似患者获得的样本的分子研究。我们在来自不同患者的五个样本中发现了扎伊尔正博拉病毒 (EBOV)。此外,我们进行了全 基因组 测序并生成了四个完整的 EBOV 基因组。这些 基因组 与来自 1976 年 Yambuku/Mayinga 暴发 的 基因组 形成一个得到良好支持的系统发育簇。这项研究表明,一个新的人畜共患 跨物种溢出 事件可能来自尚未识别的自然 储存宿主。虽然与 1976 EBOV Yambuku/Mayinga 基因组 的密切关系是惊人的,但这对动物 储存宿主 物种的理解提出了额外的挑战。该研究报告称,刚果民主共和国开赛省偏远地区出现了一种新的埃博拉病毒变种。研究结果强调需要快速检测和测序,以更好地识别变异并快速响应 暴发。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查刚果民主共和国 2025 年埃博拉病毒 暴发 的起源和遗传特征,因为该地区的 暴发 由于其快速传播的潜力和有限的医疗保健机会而经常带来重大的公共卫生挑战。
设计与对象
该研究于 2025 年 暴发 期间在刚果民主共和国开赛省布拉佩卫生区进行,该研究于 2025 年 12 月 1 日结束。研究重点是初始 暴发 阶段的六名疑似患者。
核心方法
研究人员对从六名疑似患者获得的样本进行了全基因组测序,并生成了四个完整的EBOV 基因组,以分析暴发的遗传关系和潜在起源。
主要结果
在来自不同患者的 5 个样本中鉴定出扎伊尔正埃博拉病毒 (EBOV),四个完整的 EBOV 基因组 与来自 1976 年 Yambuku/Mayinga 暴发 的 基因组 形成了一个得到充分支持的系统发育簇。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,一个新的人畜共患 跨物种溢出 事件可能来自尚未识别的天然 储存宿主,这凸显了需要进一步研究 埃博拉病毒 的起源和 传播 动力学。
科研与公卫意义
开赛省偏远地区出现的埃博拉新变种凸显了快速检测和测序对于更好地了解和应对未来 暴发 的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究由于只关注单个 暴发 且缺乏动物 储存宿主 的详细信息而受到限制,但由于对多个患者样本的分子分析,证据是强有力的。

Phylogenetic analysis of the 2025 Ebola outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo

Authors: Adrienne Amuri-Aziza, Gradi Luakanda-Ndelemo, Jean-Claude Makangara-Cigolo, Eddy Kinganda-Lusamaki, Christian Ngandu, Serge Bula-Loso, Mathias Mossoko, Louis Tshulo, Prince Akil-Bandali, Richard Kitenge-Omasumbu
Original abstract (English)
On 4 September 2025, the Ministry of Public Health, Hygiene and Social Welfare officially declared the 16th Ebola disease outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo (DRC). This outbreak ended on 1 December 2025 and occurred in Bulape Health Zone, Kasaï Province, an area with limited access to appropriate healthcare facilities and resources. Here, we describe the probable index patient and molecular investigations of samples obtained from six suspected patients from the initial outbreak phase. We identified Orthoebolavirus zairense (EBOV) in five samples from different patients. In addition, we performed whole-genome sequencing and generated four complete EBOV genomes. These genomes form a well-supported phylogenetic cluster with genomes from the 1976 Yambuku/Mayinga outbreak. This study suggests a likely new zoonotic spillover event from an as-yet unidentified natural reservoir. While the close relationship to 1976 EBOV Yambuku/Mayinga genomes is striking, this poses additional challenges on the comprehension of the animal reservoir species. The study reports the emergence of a new Ebola variant in a remote area of Kasai Province, DR Congo. The findings highlight the need for rapid detection and sequencing to better identify variants and quickly respond to outbreaks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to investigate the origin and genetic characteristics of the 2025 Ebola outbreak in the Democratic Republic of the Congo, as outbreaks in this region often pose significant public health challenges due to their potential for rapid spread and limited healthcare access.
Design and population
The study was conducted in the Bulape Health Zone, Kasaï Province, DRC, during the 2025 outbreak, which ended on 1 December 2025. It focused on six suspected patients from the initial outbreak phase.
Core methods
The researchers performed whole-genome sequencing on samples obtained from the six suspected patients and generated four complete EBOV genomes to analyze the genetic relationships and potential origins of the outbreak.
Main results
Orthoebolavirus zairense (EBOV) was identified in five samples from different patients, and the four complete EBOV genomes formed a well-supported phylogenetic cluster with genomes from the 1976 Yambuku/Mayinga outbreak.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest a likely new zoonotic spillover event from an as-yet unidentified natural reservoir, highlighting the need for further research into the origins and transmission dynamics of Ebola viruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The emergence of a new Ebola variant in a remote area of Kasai Province underscores the importance of rapid detection and sequencing to better understand and respond to future outbreaks.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its focus on a single outbreak and the lack of detailed information on the animal reservoir, but the evidence is strong due to the molecular analysis of multiple patient samples.

刚果民主共和国的埃博拉疫情可能是历史上最严重的:4项措施有助于结束疫情

DRC’s Ebola epidemic could be the worst in history: 4 things that could help end it
作者： Yap Boum, Marie Belizaire
摘要中文翻译这有助于将样本采集与结果之间的时间缩短,从超过一周缩短到仅仅几小时。刚果民主共和国(DRC)与埃博拉病毒的斗争已达到临界点。它可以通过加速目前正在进行的措施来控制最新的埃博拉疫情暴发,否则可能面临疫情持续成为有史以来最严重埃博拉疫情的风险。截至2026年9月1日,自2026年5月首次宣布以来,已报告超过6,186例确诊病例和3,007例死亡。这是刚果民主共和国历史上最致命的埃博拉疫情暴发。该疫情暴发被认为起源于刚果民主共和国东北部Ituri省Mongbwalu高流动性矿区,时间在2026年4月下旬,随后通过相互关联的社区和医疗网络传播至Ituri省的Rwampara和Bunya卫生区,以及乌干达。应对该疫情暴发的行动主要由刚果民主共和国政府主导,非洲疾病预防控制中心、世界卫生组织和其他合作伙伴支持扩大疫情暴发监测、实验室能力、疫苗接种中心、感染预防与控制、流行病学、后勤保障、社区参与以及安全和体面的葬礼。取得了显著进展,包括通过果断的国家领导和与社区的密切合作,在乌干达中断了疫情暴发。然而,在刚果民主共和国,不安全局势、人口流动性、诊断延迟、资金和物资的缺口以及社区参与不足持续维持着疫情暴发。因此,目前的应对措施尚不足以中断疫情暴发的传播...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了控制刚果民主共和国(DRC)埃博拉疫情的挑战,并强调需要改进策略以防止其成为历史上最严重的疫情。
设计与对象
该研究基于刚果民主共和国正在进行的埃博拉病毒 暴发,该病毒于 2026 年 4 月下旬在伊图里省蒙布瓦卢矿区开始,并已蔓延到鲁万帕拉、布尼亚和乌干达。它重点关注该病毒的 Bundibugyo 毒株,该毒株是 暴发 的罪魁祸首。
核心方法
这些方法涉及分析 暴发 动态,包括地理分布、人口流动性和社区参与挑战。该研究还评估了应对工作,例如 治疗 设施的建立、实验室容量的扩大以及安全埋葬实践的实施。
主要结果
截至 2026 年 9 月 1 日,已报告确诊病例超过 6,186 例,死亡病例 3,007 例。传播 在乌干达已被中断,但在刚果民主共和国,不安全、人口流动、检测延迟和社区参与不足继续维持着 暴发。有效再生数 (Rt) 从 4.0 下降到略高于 1。
解释与创新
作者解释说,有效繁殖数的下降表明应对措施取得了进展,但挑战的持续存在凸显了需要采取更多本地化和社区驱动的干预措施。新颖之处在于强调了本迪布焦毒株带来的具体困难以及解决这些因素以控制疫情的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明需要改进 监测、测试和社区参与策略,特别是在偏远和高流动性地区,以有效控制 埃博拉病毒 的 Bundibugyo 菌株。他们还强调了受影响社区附近 疫苗 配送和 治疗 容量的重要性。
局限与证据强度
证据基于报告的病例数、暴发 动态和响应工作,可能会出现检测和报告延迟的情况。由于对观测数据的依赖以及 暴发 环境的复杂性,研究结果的可信度为中等。

As at 1 September 2026, more than 6,186 confirmed cases and 3,007 deaths have been reported since May 2026 when it was first declared. It is the deadliest Ebola outbreak in the history of the DRC. The outbreak is being driven by the Bundibugyo strain of the virus that causes Ebola disease. There is currently no licensed vaccine or specific treatment for Bundibugyo. The outbreak is thought to have originated in the high-mobility mining area of Mongbwalu in Ituri, north-east DRC, in late April 2026, before spreading through interconnected communities and healthcare networks to Rwampara and Bunia – health zones in the Ituri province – and subsequently Uganda. The response to the outbreak has been nationally led by the government of the DRC, with Africa Centres for Disease Control, the World Health Organization and other partners supporting the expansion of surveillance, laboratory capacity, treatment centres, infection prevention and control, vaccination, logistics, community engagement and safe and dignified burials. Significant progress has been achieved, including interruption of transmission in Uganda through decisive national leadership and close collaboration with communities. However, in the DRC, insecurity, population mobility, delayed detection, gaps in financing and supplies, and insufficient community ownership have continued to sustain transmission. View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the challenge of controlling the Ebola epidemic in the Democratic Republic of Congo (DRC) and highlights the need for improved strategies to prevent it from becoming the worst in history.
Design and population
The study is based on the DRC’s ongoing Ebola outbreak, which began in late April 2026 in the mining area of Mongbwalu, Ituri province, and has spread to Rwampara, Bunia, and Uganda. It focuses on the Bundibugyo strain of the virus, which is responsible for the outbreak.
Core methods
The methods involve analyzing the outbreak dynamics, including the geographical spread, population mobility, and community engagement challenges. The study also evaluates the response efforts, such as the establishment of treatment facilities, expansion of laboratory capacity, and implementation of safe burial practices.
Main results
As of 1 September 2026, over 6,186 confirmed cases and 3,007 deaths have been reported. Transmission has been interrupted in Uganda, but in the DRC, insecurity, population mobility, delayed detection, and insufficient community engagement continue to sustain the outbreak. The effective reproduction number (Rt) has decreased from 4.0 to just over 1.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the decrease in the effective reproduction number indicates progress in the response, but the persistence of challenges highlights the need for more localized and community-driven interventions. The novelty lies in emphasizing the specific difficulties posed by the Bundibugyo strain and the importance of addressing these factors to control the epidemic.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for improved surveillance, testing, and community engagement strategies, particularly in remote and high-mobility areas, to effectively control the Bundibugyo strain of Ebola virus. They also highlight the importance of vaccine distribution and treatment capacity near affected communities.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on reported case numbers, outbreak dynamics, and response efforts, which may be subject to delays in detection and reporting. The confidence in the findings is moderate due to the reliance on observational data and the complexity of the outbreak environment.
